Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The electronics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 897 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 616 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the electronics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^897 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 616-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the electronics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 533. The moduli space has dimension at most 13 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 533 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the electronics structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 13. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
